<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屢次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>收點</rb><rt>siu-tin</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>Feh-leng</rt><rb>文雅</rb><rt>veng-yüô</rt><rb>粗蠻</rb><rt>tsʽu-mæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>債戶</rb><rt>tsa-wu</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盡</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>嘸周割禮</rb><rt>m-tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>*lih-fah-go*</rt><rb>文字</rb><rt>veng-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>肉身上</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>*tsiu-keh-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>*sön*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>心裏個</rb><rt>sing-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>靈氣</rb><rt>ling-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>文字</rb><rt>veng-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>稱讚個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. III.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>*ih-cʽü*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>多個</rb><rt>to-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>詔書</rb><rt>tsiao-shü</rt><rb>託付</rb><rt>tʽôh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>Gyi-cong</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嘸信實個</rb><rt>m-sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸信實</rb><rt>m-sing-jih</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>廢掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>信實</rb><rt>sing-jih</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>𣍐個</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>說謊個</rb><rt>shih-hwông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*Næn-kæn*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>動怒</rb><rt>dong-nu</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>𣍐個</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>*ziah-z*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>*tsing-dzih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，）「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>Keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公平個</rb><rt>kong-bing-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>Tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>Tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>岔路</rb><rt>tsʽô-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>變了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>無用個</rb><rt>vu-yüong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>良善</rb><rt>liang-jün</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>胡嚨</rb><rt>wu-long</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>開間個</rb><rt>kʽæ-kæn-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>單</rb><rt>*tæn*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>哄騙</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘴脣</rb><rt>cü-jing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>蛇個</rb><rt>dzô-go</rt><rb>毒氣</rb><rt>doh-kyʽi</rt><rb>囥的</rb><rt>kʽông-tih</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>滿口</rb><rt>mun-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>惡毒</rb><rt>ôh-doh</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>飛跑</rb><rt>fi-pʽao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打出</rb><rt>tang-cʽih</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下個</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>塞住</rb><rt>seh-djü</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>服罪</rb><rt>voh-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kao-hyüing. 或話，教訓。（原註）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>NÆN-KÆN</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>表出來了</rb><rt>piao-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>*shü*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>對證個</rb><rt>te-tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>眼開眼閉</rb><rt>ngæn-kʽæ-ngæn-pi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>犯過個</rb><rt>væn-ko-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>除掉了</rb><rt>djü-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>除掉個</rb><rt>*djü-diao-go*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>行爲個</rb><rt>ʽang-we-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>*fông-fah*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可見</rb><rt>kʽo-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>*sön*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>*tsing-dzih*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>廢掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lm. IV.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>*kwʽô-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？「<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>作工個</rb><rt>tsôh-kong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作工個</rb><rt>tsôh-kong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>蓋住了</rb><rt>kæ-djü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>*tæn-tsih*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>嘸周割禮個</rb><rt>m-tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>纔始</rb><rt>*dzæ-s*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>算法</rb><rt>sön-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>周割禮個</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>印證</rb><rt>ing-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>周割禮個</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>腳步</rb><rt>kyiah-bu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>落空了</rb><rt>lôh-kʽong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>變了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>嘸用場</rb><rt>m-yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>*tsʽæn-nyih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>*ʽeo-dæ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>*ʽeo-dæ*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>國份個</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸想望</rb><rt>m-siang-vông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>想望個</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>國份個</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>*Sing-shü*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>*to*</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>靠百歲</rb><rt>kʽao-pah-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>血氣</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>枯燥了</rb><rt>kwʽu-sao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>撒拉</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-lah-go</rt><rb>胎裏</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>枯燥了</rb><rt>kwʽu-sao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>疑心</rb><rt>nyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>堅固了</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>篤實</rb><rt>toh-jih</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>送掉</rb><rt>song-diao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. V.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>相和了</rb><rt>siang-ʽo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>患難</rb><rt>wæn-næn</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>高興</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>患難</rb><rt>wæn-næn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>愛惜心</rb><rt>æ-sih-sing</rt><rb>澆</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽO-hwông</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>*tsiang-læ-go*</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>相和了</rb><rt>siang-ʽo-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>和攏了</rb><rt>ʽo-long-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>Wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>高興</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>和攏個</rb><rt>ʽo-long-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>SÔ-YI</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來東了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>犯法個</rb><rt>væn-fah-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>後來</rb><rt>ʽeo-læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>印證</rb><rt>ing-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, dzæ-ü gyi. 有得翻，在於其。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>賞賜</rb><rt>sông-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>茂盛</rb><rt>meo-zing</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>出來個</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>賞賜</rb><rt>sông-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>*ko-væn*</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>恩賞</rb><rt>eng-sông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>*lih-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>*lih-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>作出來</rb><rt>tsôh-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>多個</rb><rt>to-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>多起來了</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lm. VI.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>葬好</rb><rt>tsông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>重先</rb><rt>djong-sin</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>天父個</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>合攏</rb><rt>ʽeh-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>*yiang-shih*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>合攏</rb><rt>*ʽeh-long*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>舊人</rb><rt>gyiu-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>釘煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>罪惡個</rb><rt>ze-ôh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>脫出了</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng*, tʽeh-cʽih ze sön tsing-dzih. 本文，脫出罪算正直。（原註）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>*üong-yün*</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>*sön*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>肌身個</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>私慾</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>百體</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>非禮個</rb><rt>fi-li-go</rt><rb>家生</rb><rt>kô-sang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>*pô*</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百體</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>家生</rb><rt>kô-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>Ze</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>恩惠個</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kyüing-kyʽi. 或話，君器。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>恩惠個</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>*nu-boh*</rt><rb>歸於</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>依順個</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>*nu-boh*</rt><rb>歸於</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>多謝</rb><rt>To-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>道理個</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>規模</rb><rt>kwe-mo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>釋放了</rb><rt>sih-fông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>世情</rb><rt>shü-dzing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百體</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穢污</rb><rt>w̆e-u</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>非禮</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非禮個</rb><rt>fi-li-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百體</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>惶恐個</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>dao-dzing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹個</rb><rt>z-ken-go</rt><rb>歸根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>dao-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>歸根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Tsiao* *Peng-veng*, *dao-li* *z* *tông* *su-ts*, *i-jing* *dao-li* *cü-kwu* *ziang* *yiang-ko-liao-go* *dong-shü* *tao* *læ* *su-ts* *li-hyiang* *cü-cʽih-go* *ka*. 照本文，道理是當塑子，依順道理主顧像煬過了個銅水倒來塑子裏向鑄出個介。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ih-ngæn m-neh kong-yi. 或話，一眼嘸得公義。（原註）</sup>

### Lm. VII.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>結攏了個</rb><rt>kyih-long-liao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>男𡢿個</rb><rt>nen-nyüing-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>姦婦</rb><rt>kæn-vu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>姦婦</rb><rt>kæn-vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>*yüong-wô*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肉身裏</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-li</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>發出來</rb><rt>*fah-cʽih-læ*</rt><rb>罪惡個</rb><rt>ze-ôh-go</rt><rb>私慾</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行動</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>百體</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>歸於</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>服個</rb><rt>voh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>脫出了</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>文字個</rb><rt>veng-z-go</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>靈氣個</rb><rt>ling-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>*fah-ts*</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *wô*, ah-lah z tʽeh-cʽih lih-fah, feh voh gyi kæ-kwun; ing-we, kông-tao sô kwun-djü ah-lah, z yi-kying si-de. 有得話，阿拉是脫出律法，弗服該管；因爲——講到所管住阿拉——是已經死了。（原註）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>貪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>貪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>感謝</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>*tʽeh-diao*</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. VIII.

<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>SÔ-YI</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>毫無</rb><rt>ʽao-vu</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>嘸力量</rb><rt>m-lih-liang</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>*yi-kying*</rt><rb>做過了</rb><rt>*tso-ko-de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>成了</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>有罪個</rb><rt>yiu-ze-go</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪個</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>*liu-sing*</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>留心</rb><rt>Liu-sing</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>*voh*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, we-leh ng-lah li-hyiang deng-tih Gyi-zi-go Ling, we s-teh ng-lah iao si go kyi-sing yia hao weh. 或話，爲了爾拉裏向庉的其自個靈，會使得爾拉要死個肌身也好活。（原註）</sup>

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>KA</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>肌身個</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>私慾</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>領子個</rb><rt>ling-ts-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>阿爸</rb><rt>Ah-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>*tso-leh*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接代個</rb><rt>tsih-dæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>接代</rb><rt>tsih-dæ</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>接代個</rb><rt>tsih-dæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>同</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>*tsiang-læ*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>同</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>享</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>今世個</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt><rb>比弗得</rb><rt>pi-feh-teh</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>Zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>Zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>虛花</rb><rt>hyü-hwô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>情願個</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>*næn-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>兒囡個</rb><rt>ng-nô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>釋放個</rb><rt>sih-fông-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>嗐聲嘆氣</rb><rt>ʽæ-sing-tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>生產個</rb><rt>seng-tsʽæn-go</rt><rb>苦頭</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>嗐聲嘆氣</rb><rt>ʽæ-sing-tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>領子個</rb><rt>ling-ts-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>取贖個</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>眼見個</rb><rt>ngæn-kyin-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。
