<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>軟弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>求法</rb><rt>gyiu-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>講弗出個</rb><rt>kông-feh-cʽih-go</rt><rb>嘆息</rb><rt>tʽæn-sih</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing-Ling*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>*ts-i*</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>歸攏</rb><rt>kwe-long</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>形象</rb><rt>ying-ziang</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>長子</rb><rt>Tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定規個</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>Bing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> 24</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>富足個</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>*soh-go*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>*ni*</rt></ruby>？

「<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>從前</rb><rt>*dzong-zin*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>正直無私</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-vu-s</rt><rb>了結</rb><rt>liao-kyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>*Yi-seh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>了結</rb><rt>liao-kyih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上文</rb><rt>zông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>天兵天將個</rb><rt>tʽin-ping-tʽin-tsiang-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>變了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Iao kyüih-tön *Gyi-go* su-moh. 或話，要決斷其個數目。（原註）</sup>

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>追求</rb><rt>tse-gyiu</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>追求</rb><rt>tse-gyiu</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>趕着</rb><rt>ken-djôh</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>追求</rb><rt>*tse-gyiu*</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撞着</rb><rt>djông-djôh</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>𢲔跌個</rb><rt>pæn-tih-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>𢲔跌個</rb><rt>pæn-tih-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>關礙</rb><rt>kwæn-ngæ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>郇</u>山上</rb><rt>Shing-sæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」 

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tao-me. 或話，倒霉。（原註）</sup>

### Lm. X.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心裏個</rb><rt>sing-li-go</rt><rb>願頭</rb><rt>nyün-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt><rb>熱心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>理路</rb><rt>li-lu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>清</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>歸根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>I-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>？』（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶落來</rb><rt>ta-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。）<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>陰司裏</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt></ruby>？』（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>帶上來</rb><rt>ta-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。）<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？『<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>近個</rb><rt>gying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>。』 」<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>萬國人個</rb><rt>væn-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>獨一位</rb><rt>doh-ih-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>富足個</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>*eng-we*</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>Wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>？<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>差來</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>和平個</rb><rt>ʽo-bing-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>美勒緊</rb><rt>me-leh-kying</rt></ruby>！」 

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳個</rb><rt>djün-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>？」 

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳個</rb><rt>djün-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳個</rb><rt>djün-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>聽聞過了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>行遍</rb><rt>ʽang-pin</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>開家</rb><rt>Kʽæ-kô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>激動</rb><rt>kyih-dong</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>激起</rb><rt>kyih-kyʽi</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>怒心</rb><rt>nu-sing</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>膽子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>碰着了</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打聽</rb><rt>tang-tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看出了</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>強辯個</rb><rt>gyiang-bin-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」 

---

### Lm. XI.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>Bing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>便雅憫</u>個</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇篇</rb><rt>*keh-pʽin*</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>剩勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謀害</rb><rt>meo-ʽæ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七千</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>留落東</rb><rt>liu-lôh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力</rb><rt>Pô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>*bu-sah*</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>剩落東個</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>追求個</rb><rt>tse-gyiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>懵懂</rb><rt>mong-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>桌凳</rb><rt>coh-teng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>𢲔馬索</rb><rt>pæn-mô-sôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>關礙</rb><rt>kwæn-ngæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>還報</rb><rt>wæn-pao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>背脊</rb><rt>pe-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>壓勒</rb><rt>ah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>直弗起</rb><rt>*dzih-feh-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, heh-ngæn. 有得翻，瞎眼。（原註）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>至於</rb><rt>ts-ü</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>Bing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>錯誤</rb><rt>tsʽôh-ngwu</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>*ling-djôh*</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇向</rb><rt>*keh-hyiang*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>激起</rb><rt>kyih-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>錯誤</rb><rt>tsʽôh-ngwu</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>缺欠</rb><rt>kyʽüih-kyʽin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看重</rb><rt>kʽen-djong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>激起</rb><rt>kyih-kyʽi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>親</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>骨肉</rb><rt>kweh-joh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚且</rb><rt>jông-tsʽia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>和攏</rb><rt>ʽo-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>死之中個</rb><rt>si-ts-cong-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>獻新個</rb><rt>Hyin-sing-go</rt><rb>麥粉</rb><rt>*mah-feng*</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和團</rb><rt>ʽo-dön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起了個</rb><rt>*tseng-kyʽi-liao-go*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>樹個</rb><rt>*jü-go*</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起了個</rb><rt>*tseng-kyʽi-liao-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾根</rb><rt>kyi-keng</rt><rb>斷落了</rb><rt>dön-lôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>野</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>接上</rb><rt>tsih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>漿水</rb><rt>tsiang-shü</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>*tsʽeng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>*kʽao-djôh*</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyüong-lôh-kyʽi. 或話，窮落去。（原註）</sup>

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特意</rb><rt>deh-i</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>斷落</rb><rt>dön-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>接上</rb><rt>tsih-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>原是</rb><rt>Nyün-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>斷落</rb><rt>dön-lôh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>心高氣傲</rb><rt>sing-kao-kyʽi-ngao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>生成個</rb><rt>sang-dzing-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>慈愛</rb><rt>dz-æ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>嚴厲</rb><rt>nyin-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>跌落個</rb><rt>tih-lôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>嚴厲</rb><rt>nyin-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>慈愛</rb><rt>dz-æ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>感念</rb><rt>ken-nyiæn</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>慈愛</rb><rt>dz-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>斫掉</rb><rt>tsôh-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>終於</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接上</rb><rt>tsih-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>接上</rb><rt>tsih-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>本底子</rb><rt>peng-ti-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>野</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>*ô-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>斫落仔</rb><rt>tsôh-lôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>*ng-go*</rt><rb>底子</rb><rt>ti-ts</rt><rb>接勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>本底子</rb><rt>peng-ti-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>*ken-læn*</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>*jü-go*</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>*ô-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>幾分</rb><rt>kyi-feng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>滿足個</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>救個</rb><rt>Kyiu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>郇</u>山</rb><rt>Shing-sæn</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>寵愛</rb><rt>cʽong-æ</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>*jü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>*jü*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>反悔</rb><rt>fæn-hwe</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>攔進</rb><rt>lah-tsing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, ngæn-tsing hah-de. 有得翻，眼睛瞎了。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Keh* *liang-tsih*, feh i-jing, *sæn-go-z*, *yiu-teh* *fæn*, feh siang-sing. 箇兩節，「弗依順」有得翻，「弗相信」。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, pô gyi long-tsong dô-kô lah-tsing. 或話，把其攏總大家攔進。（原註）</sup>

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>無窮</rb><rt>vu-gyüong</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>追究</rb><rt>tse-kyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>路道</rb><rt>lu-dao</rt><rb>測度弗到個</rb><rt>tsʽeh-dôh-feh-tao-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>商量過</rb><rt>siang-liang-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>35</sup> 「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>*soh-si*</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>報還</rb><rt>pao-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>本於</rb><rt>peng-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸於</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>*Dæn-nyün*</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>*kwe*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lm. XII.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>*ih-tsʽih-go*</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>靈活</rb><rt>ling-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世個</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>變了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>換過</rb><rt>wun-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辨出</rb><rt>bin-cʽih</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全備個</rb><rt>djün-be-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>過於</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>合理個</rb><rt>ʽeh-li-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>肢體</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>肢體</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>互相</rb><rt>ngwu-siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>百體</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>*kôh-nying*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>先知個</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>分寸</rb><rt>feng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>*sin-cü-nying*</rt></ruby>；<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>辦事個</rb><rt>bæn-z-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>*eng-s*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>辦事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>；<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>勸化個</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>勸化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>勤力</rb><rt>gying-lih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>歡喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kwʽun-ʽong-da-liang: *kʽen* 2K. 8. 2. 或話，寬宏大量。看 2K. 8. 2.（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>Jing-æ</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>假個</rb><rt>kô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>親愛</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>切心</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相愛</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>尊敬</rb><rt>tseng-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>推尊</rb><rt>tʽe-tseng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>推尊</rb><rt>tʽe-tseng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>勤力</rb><rt>gying-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>懈怠</rb><rt>yiæ-dæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt><rb>熱心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>*z-djông*</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>存</rb><rt>Dzeng</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>高興</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>患難</rb><rt>wæn-næn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>長遠之</rb><rt>dziang-yün-ts</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>接待</rb><rt>tsih-dæ</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殷勤</rb><rt>ing-gying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>悲傷個</rb><rt>pe-shông-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>驕傲個</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>低微個</rb><rt>ti-vi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>同行同坐</rb><rt>dong-ʽang-dong-zo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, cün-kwun ti-vi-go z-ken. 或話，專管低微個事幹。（原註）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>Ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>體面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>做勒來</rb><rt>tso-leh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>儘</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>Tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>推讓</rb><rt>tʽe-nyiang</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>Sing-ün</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>還報</rb><rt>wæn-pao</rt></ruby>。』」 <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可比</rb><rt>*kʽo-pi*</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>炭火</rb><rt>tʽæn-ho</rt><rb>堆上</rb><rt>te-zông</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭頂</rb><rt>deo-teng</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>打輸</rb><rt>tang-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打贏</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lm. XIII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>在上</rb><rt>dzæ-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>服其</rb><rt>voh-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>拒絕</rb><rt>gyü-djih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>抵敵個</rb><rt>ti-dih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>*pʽô*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>善</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>*pʽô*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>良心上</rb><rt>liang-sing-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>應得個</rb><rt>ing-teh-go</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>應得</rb><rt>*ing-teh*</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>*kwe*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>應得</rb><rt>*ing-teh*</rt><rb>稅鈿</rb><rt>se-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>稅鈿</rb><rt>se-din</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>應得</rb><rt>*ing-teh*</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>應得</rb><rt>*ing-teh*</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. XIV.

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>菜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>喫個</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>看重</rb><rt>kʽen-djong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>看重</rb><rt>kʽen-djong</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>顧着</rb><rt>Kwu-djôh</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>顧着個</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>顧着個</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>喫個</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>活人個</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>輕看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>法堂前</rb><rt>fah-dông-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>*Sing-shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』」 <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>賬</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>𢲔跌個</rb><rt>pæn-tih-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>關礙</rb><rt>kwæn-ngæ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>箇樣</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>*ng-go*</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Feh kyih-zing; *ʽôh* *wô*, ze-djông-go. 弗潔淨。或話，隨常個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>稱讚個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>關於</rb><rt>kwæn-ü</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>當心</rb><rt>tông-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>Yiang-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>帶累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>葷</rb><rt>hweng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>*ze-bin*</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>*soh-go*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害累</rb><rt>ʽæ-li</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>礙</rb><rt>ngæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>作興</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>見疑</rb><rt>kyin-nyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. XV.

<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>單</rb><rt>*tæn*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>善</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>訓誨</rb><rt>hyüing-hwe</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>想望個</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>榜樣</rb><rt>*pông-yiang*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一心一意</rb><rt>ih-sing-ih-i</rt><rb>合口齊動</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, *feng-fu*. 或話，吩咐。（原註）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>周割禮個</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>辦事人</rb><rt>Bæn-z-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>；<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>*s-teh*</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>。」
