<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb><u>耶西</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-si-go</rt><rb>根株</rb><rt>Keng-cü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>仰望</rb><rt>nyiang-vông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>想望個</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>良善</rb><rt>liang-jün</rt><rb>來勒</rb><rt>læ-leh</rt><rb>足</rb><rt>tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相勸</rb><rt>siang-kyʽün</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放膽點</rb><rt>fông-tæn-tin</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>提醒</rb><rt>di-sing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby> <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>辦事人</rb><rt>bæn-z-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>福音個</rb><rt>foh-ing-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>尊起了個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有勒</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>*kông*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>藉</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>*yüong*</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>*ngô-go*</rt><rb>口供</rb><rt>kʽeo-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行止</rb><rt>ʽang-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>奇事個</rb><rt>gyi-z-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈個</rb><rt>Ling-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利哩古</rb><rt>Yi-li-li-kwu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>傳遍</rb><rt>djün-pin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>相</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>牆腳上</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>*Sing-shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>風聲</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>遭數</rb><rt>tsao-su</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>嘸處</rb><rt>m-cʽü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>*djün*</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>*foh-ing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>屢年</rb><rt>le-nyin</rt><rb>渴想</rb><rt>kʽeh-siang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士班雅</rb><rt>Z-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>順路</rb><rt>jing-lu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>聚聚</rb><rt>*jü-jü*</rt><rb>有點</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>依心滿足</rb><rt>i-sing-mun-tsoh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, sông-sông-kwʽa-kwʽa jü-jü. 或話，爽爽快快聚聚。（原註）</sup>

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> ### Lm. XVI.

<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓比</rb><rt>Fi-pi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>堅革哩</rb><rt>Kyin-keh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>辦事人</rb><rt>bæn-z-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>託託</rb><rt>tʽôh-tʽôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>*pông-dzu*</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Keh* *liang* *tsih*, keh-kô *liang-go* *z*, *yiu-teh* *wô*, beng-yiu. 箇兩節，「箇家」兩個字，有得話，「朋友」。（原註）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>學過個</rb><rt>ʽôh-ko-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>害累</rb><rt>ʽæ-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>避開</rb><rt>bi-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇株</rb><rt>Keh-cü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>*voh-z*</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>甜糖蜜渧</rb><rt>din-dông-mih-ti</rt><rb>奉承</rb><rt>vong-dzing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>哄誘</rb><rt>hong-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>依順個</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>*ming-sing*</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>老實</rb><rt>lao-jih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>和平個</rb><rt>ʽo-bing-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>踏糊</rb><rt>dah-wu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nyü-beng. 或話，愚笨。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, sông-ʽæ læ ng-lah kyiah-ʽô: *kʽen* Ts. 3. 15. 或話，傷害來爾拉腳下。看 Ts. 3. 15.（原註）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>幫手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路求</rb><rt>Lu-gyiu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶孫</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>所西巴德</rb><rt>Sô-si-pô-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>代寫</rb><rt>dæ-sia</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>德丟</rb><rt>Teh-tiu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦猶</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教會裏個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li-go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>縣裏個</rb><rt>Yün-li-go</rt><rb>庫房</rb><rt>kwʽu-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拉都</rb><rt>Yi-lah-tu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>括土</rb><rt>Kwah-tʽu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永古以來</rb><rt>üong-kwu-yi-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>隱藏的</rb><rt>ing-dzông-tih</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>表</rb><rt>piao</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>萬國人</rb><rt>væn-koh-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt></ruby>。）<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>獨一位</rb><rt>doh-ih-we</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
